Window intervals are half-open `[start, start + 60s)` (start inclusive, end exclusive), so an ordinary reading falls into exactly two overlapping windows. Only windows that contain at least one reading are emitted.\n"[39m +
                [32m'* Per-window profile statistics (computed per sensor, per window): `count` = number of readings in the window; `mean` = arithmetic mean of the values; `variance` = **population** variance (sum of squared deviations divided by `count`); `std` = square root of the population variance.\n'[39m +
                [32m"* Anomaly rule: for each reading in each window, its z-score is `(value - mean) / std` using that window's `mean` and population `std`. Because windows overlap, the same reading may be flagged in more than one window (once per qualifying window).\n"[39m +
                [32m'* Threshold: read the float threshold from the environment variable `ZSCORE_THRESHOLD`; when it is unset, default to `3.0`.\n'[39m +

                    [32m'\r\n'[39m +
                    [32m'    """\r\n'[39m +
                    [32m'\r\n'[39m +
                    [32m'    def __init__(\r\n'[39m +
                    [32m'        self,\r\n'[39m +
                    [32m'        path: Union[Path, str],\r\n'[39m +
                    [32m'        batch_size: int = 1000,\r\n'[39m +
                    [32m'        get_fs_id: Callable[[Path], str] = _get_path_dev,\r\n'[39m +
                    [32m'    ):\r\n'[39m +
                    [32m'        """Init.\r\n'[39m +
                    [32m'\r\n'[39m +
                    [32m'        :arg path: Path to file.\r\n'[39m +
                    [32m'\r\n'[39m +
                    [32m'        :arg batch_size: Number of lines to read per batch. Defaults\r\n'[39m +
                    [32m'            to 1000.\r\n'[39m +
                    [32m'\r\n'[39m +
                    [32m'        :arg get_fs_id: Called with the parent directory and must\r\n'[39m +
                    [32m'            return a consistent (across workers and restarts) unique\r\n'[39m +
                    [32m'            ID for the filesystem of that directory. Defaults to using\r\n'[39m +
                    [32m'            {py:obj}`os.stat_result.st_dev`.\r\n'[39m +
                    [32m'\r\n'[39m +
                    [32m'            If you know all workers have access to identical files,\r\n'[39m +
                    [32m'            you can have this return a constant: `lambda _dir:\r\n'[39m +
                    [32m'            "SHARED"`.\r\n'[39m +
                    [32m'\r\n'[39m +
                    [32m'        """\r\n'[39m +
                    [32m'        if not isinstance(path, Path):\r\n'[39m +
                    [32m'            path = Path(path)\r\n'[39m +
                    [32m'\r\n'[39m +
                    [32m'        self._path = path\r\n'[39m +
                    [32m'        self._batch_size = batch_size\r\n'[39m +
                    [32m'        self._fs_id = get_fs_id(path.parent)\r\n'[39m +
                    [32m'        if "::" in self._fs_id:\r\n'[39m +
                    [32m'            msg = f"result of `get_fs_id` must not contain `::`; got {self._fs_id!r}"\r\n'[39m +
                    [32m'            raise ValueError(msg)\r\n'[39m +
                    [32m'\r\n'[39m +
                    [32m'    @override\r\n'[39m +
                    [32m'    def list_parts(self) -> List[str]:\r\n'[39m +
                    [32m'        if self._path.exists():\r\n'[39m +

                    [32m'        else:\r\n'[39m +

                    [32m'        return _FileSourcePartition(self._path, self._batch_size, resume_state)\r\n'[39m +
                    [32m'\r\n'[39m +
                    [32m''[39m,
                  isTruncated: [33mfalse[39m

                  [32m'\n'[39m +
                  [32m'Reads per-sensor readings from a JSONL file, computes per-sensor\n'[39m +
                  [32m'per-window statistical profiles over 60s sliding windows (30s slide,\n'[39m +
                  [32m'aligned to the Unix epoch), flags readings whose absolute z-score\n'[39m +

                  [32m'threshold, and writes both the window profiles and the flagged\n'[39m +

                  [32m'kept by this pipeline (plain lists of (int, float) tuples) is fully\n'[39m +
                  [32m'picklable.\n'[39m +
                  [32m'"""\n'[39m +
                  [32m'\n'[39m +
                  [32m'import json\n'[39m +
                  [32m'import os\n'[39m +
                  [32m'from datetime import datetime, timedelta, timezone\n'[39m +
                  [32m'from pathlib import Path\n'[39m +
                  [32m'from typing import Any, Dict, List, Tuple\n'[39m +

                  [32m'ANOMALIES_PATH = OUTPUT_DIR / "anomalies.jsonl"\n'[39m +
                  [32m'\n'[39m +
                  [32m'OUTPUT_DIR.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)\n'[39m +
                  [32m'\n'[39m +
                  [32m'WINDOW_LENGTH = timedelta(seconds=60)\n'[39m +
                  [32m'WINDOW_SLIDE = timedelta(seconds=30)\n'[39m +
                  [32m'EPOCH = datetime(1970, 1, 1, tzinfo=timezone.utc)\n'[39m +
                  [32m'SLIDE_SECONDS = int(WINDOW_SLIDE.total_seconds())\n'[39m +

                  [32m'\n'[39m +
                  [32m'Reading = Tuple[int, float]  # (epoch_seconds, value)\n'[39m +
                  [32m'\n'[39m +
                  [32m'\n'[39m +
                  [32m'def parse_line(line: str) -> Dict[str, Any]:\n'[39m +
                  [32m'    """Parse a raw JSONL line into a record with an epoch-second ts."""\n'[39m +
                  [32m'    obj = json.loads(line)\n'[39m +
                  [32m'    ts = datetime.fromisoformat(obj["ts"])\n'[39m +
                  [32m'    if ts.tzinfo is None:\n'[39m +
                  [32m'        ts = ts.replace(tzinfo=timezone.utc)\n'[39m +
                  [32m'    epoch_seconds = int((ts - EPOCH).total_seconds())\n'[39m +

                  [32m'    }\n'[39m +
                  [32m'\n'[39m +
                  [32m'\n'[39m +
                  [32m'def key_by_sensor(record: Dict[str, Any]) -> str:\n'[39m +

                  [32m'\n'[39m +
                  [32m'\n'[39m +

                  [32m'    mean = sum(values) / count\n'[39m +
                  [32m'    variance = sum((v - mean) ** 2 for v in values) / count\n'[39m +
                  [32m'    std = variance ** 0.5\n'[39m +
                  [32m'    return count, mean, variance, std\n'[39m +

                  [32m'    window_end = window_start + LENGTH_SECONDS\n'[39m +
                  [32m'    values = [v for _, v in readings]\n'[39m +
                  [32m'    count, mean, variance, std = _stats(values)\n'[39m +
                  [32m'    return {\n'[39m +
                  [32m'        "sensor_id": sensor_id,\n'[39m +
                  [32m'        "window_start": window_start,\n'[39m +
                  [32m'        "window_end": window_end,\n'[39m +
                  [32m'        "count": count,\n'[39m +
                  [32m'        "mean": round(mean, 6),\n'[39m +
                  [32m'        "variance": round(variance, 6),\n'[39m +
                  [32m'        "std": round(std, 6),\n'[39m +
                  [32m'    }\n'[39m +
                  [32m'\n'[39m +
                  [32m'\n'[39m +
                  [32m'def compute_anomalies(\n'[39m +

                  [32m'    values = [v for _, v in readings]\n'[39m +
                  [32m'    _count, mean, _variance, std = _stats(values)\n'[39m +

                  [32m'    anomalies = []\n'[39m +

                  [32m'                }\n'[39m +
                  [32m'            )\n'[39m +
                  [32m'    return anomalies\n'[39m +
                  [32m'\n'[39m +
                  [32m'\n'[39m +
                  [32m'def to_json_line(obj: Dict[str, Any]) -> str:\n'[39m +
                  [32m'    return json.dumps(obj)\n'[39m +

                  [32m'\n'[39m +
                  [32m'flow = Dataflow("sensor_anomaly_detection")\n'[39m +
                  [32m'\n'[39m +
                  [32m'lines = op.input("read_lines", flow, FileSource(str(INPUT_PATH)))\n'[39m +
                  [32m'records = op.map("parse_line", lines, parse_line)\n'[39m +
                  [32m'keyed = op.key_on("key_by_sensor", records, key_by_sensor)\n'[39m +
                  [32m'\n'[39m +
                  [32m'clock = EventClock(ts_getter=get_ts, wait_for_system_duration=timedelta(seconds=0))\n'[39m +

                  [32m'\n'[39m +
                  [32m'window_out = fold_window(\n'[39m +
                  [32m'    "sliding_window",\n'[39m +

                    [32m'\r\n'[39m +
                    [32m'    """\r\n'[39m +
                    [32m'\r\n'[39m +
                    [32m'    def __init__(\r\n'[39m +
                    [32m'        self,\r\n'[39m +
                    [32m'        path: Union[Path, str],\r\n'[39m +
                    [32m'        batch_size: int = 1000,\r\n'[39m +
                    [32m'        get_fs_id: Callable[[Path], str] = _get_path_dev,\r\n'[39m +
                    [32m'    ):\r\n'[39m +
                    [32m'        """Init.\r\n'[39m +
                    [32m'\r\n'[39m +
                    [32m'        :arg path: Path to file.\r\n'[39m +
                    [32m'\r\n'[39m +
                    [32m'        :arg batch_size: Number of lines to read per batch. Defaults\r\n'[39m +
                    [32m'            to 1000.\r\n'[39m +
                    [32m'\r\n'[39m +
                    [32m'        :arg get_fs_id: Called with the parent directory and must\r\n'[39m +
                    [32m'            return a consistent (across workers and restarts) unique\r\n'[39m +
                    [32m'            ID for the filesystem of that directory. Defaults to using\r\n'[39m +
                    [32m'            {py:obj}`os.stat_result.st_dev`.\r\n'[39m +
                    [32m'\r\n'[39m +
                    [32m'            If you know all workers have access to identical files,\r\n'[39m +
                    [32m'            you can have this return a constant: `lambda _dir:\r\n'[39m +
                    [32m'            "SHARED"`.\r\n'[39m +
                    [32m'\r\n'[39m +
                    [32m'        """\r\n'[39m +
                    [32m'        if not isinstance(path, Path):\r\n'[39m +
                    [32m'            path = Path(path)\r\n'[39m +
                    [32m'\r\n'[39m +
                    [32m'        self._path = path\r\n'[39m +
                    [32m'        self._batch_size = batch_size\r\n'[39m +
                    [32m'        self._fs_id = get_fs_id(path.parent)\r\n'[39m +
                    [32m'        if "::" in self._fs_id:\r\n'[39m +
                    [32m'            msg = f"result of `get_fs_id` must not contain `::`; got {self._fs_id!r}"\r\n'[39m +
                    [32m'            raise ValueError(msg)\r\n'[39m +
                    [32m'\r\n'[39m +
                    [32m'    @override\r\n'[39m +
                    [32m'    def list_parts(self) -> List[str]:\r\n'[39m +
                    [32m'        if self._path.exists():\r\n'[39m +

                    [32m'        else:\r\n'[39m +

                    [32m'        return _FileSourcePartition(self._path, self._batch_size, resume_state)\r\n'[39m +
                    [32m'\r\n'[39m +
                    [32m''[39m,
                  isTruncated: [33mfalse[39m

                  [32m'\n'[39m +
                  [32m'Reads per-sensor readings from a JSONL file, computes per-sensor\n'[39m +
                  [32m'per-window statistical profiles over 60s sliding windows (30s slide,\n'[39m +
                  [32m'aligned to the Unix epoch), flags readings whose absolute z-score\n'[39m +

                  [32m'threshold, and writes both the window profiles and the flagged\n'[39m +

                  [32m'kept by this pipeline (plain lists of (int, float) tuples) is fully\n'[39m +
                  [32m'picklable.\n'[39m +
                  [32m'"""\n'[39m +
                  [32m'\n'[39m +
                  [32m'import json\n'[39m +
                  [32m'import os\n'[39m +
                  [32m'from datetime import datetime, timedelta, timezone\n'[39m +
                  [32m'from pathlib import Path\n'[39m +
                  [32m'from typing import Any, Dict, List, Tuple\n'[39m +

                  [32m'ANOMALIES_PATH = OUTPUT_DIR / "anomalies.jsonl"\n'[39m +
                  [32m'\n'[39m +
                  [32m'OUTPUT_DIR.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)\n'[39m +
                  [32m'\n'[39m +
                  [32m'WINDOW_LENGTH = timedelta(seconds=60)\n'[39m +
                  [32m'WINDOW_SLIDE = timedelta(seconds=30)\n'[39m +
                  [32m'EPOCH = datetime(1970, 1, 1, tzinfo=timezone.utc)\n'[39m +
                  [32m'SLIDE_SECONDS = int(WINDOW_SLIDE.total_seconds())\n'[39m +

                  [32m'\n'[39m +
                  [32m'Reading = Tuple[int, float]  # (epoch_seconds, value)\n'[39m +
                  [32m'\n'[39m +
                  [32m'\n'[39m +
                  [32m'def parse_line(line: str) -> Dict[str, Any]:\n'[39m +
                  [32m'    """Parse a raw JSONL line into a record with an epoch-second ts."""\n'[39m +
                  [32m'    obj = json.loads(line)\n'[39m +
                  [32m'    ts = datetime.fromisoformat(obj["ts"])\n'[39m +
                  [32m'    if ts.tzinfo is None:\n'[39m +
                  [32m'        ts = ts.replace(tzinfo=timezone.utc)\n'[39m +
                  [32m'    epoch_seconds = int((ts - EPOCH).total_seconds())\n'[39m +

                  [32m'    }\n'[39m +
                  [32m'\n'[39m +
                  [32m'\n'[39m +
                  [32m'def key_by_sensor(record: Dict[str, Any]) -> str:\n'[39m +

                  [32m'\n'[39m +
                  [32m'\n'[39m +

                  [32m'    mean = sum(values) / count\n'[39m +
                  [32m'    variance = sum((v - mean) ** 2 for v in values) / count\n'[39m +
                  [32m'    std = variance ** 0.5\n'[39m +
                  [32m'    return count, mean, variance, std\n'[39m +

                  [32m'    window_end = window_start + LENGTH_SECONDS\n'[39m +
                  [32m'    values = [v for _, v in readings]\n'[39m +
                  [32m'    count, mean, variance, std = _stats(values)\n'[39m +
                  [32m'    return {\n'[39m +
                  [32m'        "sensor_id": sensor_id,\n'[39m +
                  [32m'        "window_start": window_start,\n'[39m +
                  [32m'        "window_end": window_end,\n'[39m +
                  [32m'        "count": count,\n'[39m +
                  [32m'        "mean": round(mean, 6),\n'[39m +
                  [32m'        "variance": round(variance, 6),\n'[39m +
                  [32m'        "std": round(std, 6),\n'[39m +
                  [32m'    }\n'[39m +
                  [32m'\n'[39m +
                  [32m'\n'[39m +
                  [32m'def compute_anomalies(\n'[39m +

                  [32m'    values = [v for _, v in readings]\n'[39m +
                  [32m'    _count, mean, _variance, std = _stats(values)\n'[39m +

                  [32m'    anomalies = []\n'[39m +

                  [32m'                }\n'[39m +
                  [32m'            )\n'[39m +
                  [32m'    return anomalies\n'[39m +
                  [32m'\n'[39m +
                  [32m'\n'[39m +
                  [32m'def to_json_line(obj: Dict[str, Any]) -> str:\n'[39m +
                  [32m'    return json.dumps(obj)\n'[39m +

                  [32m'\n'[39m +
                  [32m'flow = Dataflow("sensor_anomaly_detection")\n'[39m +
                  [32m'\n'[39m +
                  [32m'lines = op.input("read_lines", flow, FileSource(str(INPUT_PATH)))\n'[39m +
                  [32m'records = op.map("parse_line", lines, parse_line)\n'[39m +
                  [32m'keyed = op.key_on("key_by_sensor", records, key_by_sensor)\n'[39m +
                  [32m'\n'[39m +
                  [32m'clock = EventClock(ts_getter=get_ts, wait_for_system_duration=timedelta(seconds=0))\n'[39m +

                  [32m'\n'[39m +
                  [32m'window_out = fold_window(\n'[39m +
                  [32m'    "sliding_window",\n'[39m +
